CREATE TABLE assignment_submissions (
	id SERIAL NOT NULL, 
	assignment_id INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	student_id INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	file_url VARCHAR(500), 
	content TEXT, 
	submitted_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL, 
	status submission_status_enum NOT NULL, 
	score FLOAT, 
	feedback TEXT, 
	graded_by INTEGER, 
	graded_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_assignment_student_submission UNIQUE (assignment_id, student_id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(assignment_id) REFERENCES assignments (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(student_id) REFERENCES student_profiles (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(graded_by) REFERENCES lecturer_profiles (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE users (
	id SERIAL NOT NULL, 
	email VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	full_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	role user_role_enum NOT NULL, 
	is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL, 
	created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL, 
	updated_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id)
)

CREATE TABLE attendance_records (
	id SERIAL NOT NULL, 
	session_id INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	student_id INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	status attendance_status_enum NOT NULL, 
	method attendance_method_enum, 
	check_in_time TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE, 
	catatan TEXT, 
	dioverride_oleh INTEGER, 
	dioverride_pada TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_session_student_attendance UNIQUE (session_id, student_id),

CREATE TABLE attendance_events (
	id SERIAL NOT NULL, 
	device_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
	device_db_id INTEGER, 
	session_id INTEGER, 
	student_id INTEGER, 
	event_type attendance_event_type_enum NOT NULL, 
	rfid_uid VARCHAR(32), 
	fingerprint_slot INTEGER, 
	fingerprint_confidence FLOAT, 
	event_uuid VARCHAR(64), 
	berhasil BOOLEAN NOT NULL, 
	pesan TEXT, 
	device_timestamp TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE, 
	received_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(device_db_id) REFERENCES sadita_devices (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(session_id) REFERENCES attendance_sessions (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(student_id) REFERENCES student_profiles (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE attendance_requests (
	id SERIAL NOT NULL, 
	session_id INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	student_id INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	status attendance_request_status_enum NOT NULL, 
	submitted_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL, 
	verified_by INTEGER, 
	verified_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE, 
	approved_by INTEGER, 
	approved_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE, 
	rejection_reason TEXT, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(session_id) REFERENCES attendance_sessions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(student_id) REFERENCES student_profiles (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(verified_by) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(approved_by) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

	session_id INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	student_id INTEGER NOT NULL,

	submitted_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL,

	FOREIGN KEY(student_id) REFERENCES student_profiles (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,

	FOREIGN KEY(approved_by) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE assignment_submissions (
	id SERIAL NOT NULL, 
	assignment_id INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	student_id INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	file_url VARCHAR(500), 
	content TEXT, 
	submitted_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL, 
	status submission_status_enum NOT NULL, 
	score FLOAT, 
	feedback TEXT, 
	graded_by INTEGER, 
	graded_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_assignment_student_submission UNIQUE (assignment_id, student_id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(assignment_id) REFERENCES assignments (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(student_id) REFERENCES student_profiles (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(graded_by) REFERENCES lecturer_profiles (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE attendance_events (
	id SERIAL NOT NULL, 
	device_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
	device_db_id INTEGER, 
	session_id INTEGER, 
	student_id INTEGER, 
	event_type attendance_event_type_enum NOT NULL, 
	rfid_uid VARCHAR(32), 
	fingerprint_slot INTEGER, 
	fingerprint_confidence FLOAT, 
	event_uuid VARCHAR(64), 
	berhasil BOOLEAN NOT NULL, 
	pesan TEXT, 
	device_timestamp TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE, 
	received_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(device_db_id) REFERENCES sadita_devices (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(session_id) REFERENCES attendance_sessions (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(student_id) REFERENCES student_profiles (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE course_materials (
	id SERIAL NOT NULL, 
	class_id INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	session_id INTEGER, 
	title VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL, 
	description TEXT, 
	material_type material_type_enum NOT NULL, 
	resource_url VARCHAR(500), 
	content TEXT, 
	created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL, 
	updated_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(class_id) REFERENCES course_classes (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(session_id) REFERENCES attendance_sessions (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE assignments (
	id SERIAL NOT NULL, 
	class_id INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	session_id INTEGER, 
	title VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL, 
	description TEXT, 
	due_date TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL, 
	max_score FLOAT NOT NULL, 
	allow_late BOOLEAN NOT NULL, 
	created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(class_id) REFERENCES course_classes (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(session_id) REFERENCES attendance_sessions (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE attendance_requests (
	id SERIAL NOT NULL, 
	session_id INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	student_id INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	status attendance_request_status_enum NOT NULL, 
	submitted_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL, 
	verified_by INTEGER, 
	verified_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE, 
	approved_by INTEGER, 
	approved_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE, 
	rejection_reason TEXT, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(session_id) REFERENCES attendance_sessions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(student_id) REFERENCES student_profiles (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(verified_by) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(approved_by) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE assignment_submissions (
	id SERIAL NOT NULL, 
	assignment_id INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	student_id INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	file_url VARCHAR(500), 
	content TEXT, 
	submitted_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL, 
	status submission_status_enum NOT NULL, 
	score FLOAT, 
	feedback TEXT, 
	graded_by INTEGER, 
	graded_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_assignment_student_submission UNIQUE (assignment_id, student_id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(assignment_id) REFERENCES assignments (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(student_id) REFERENCES student_profiles (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(graded_by) REFERENCES lecturer_profiles (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)
